22	const ICON_CLOSE = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M19 6.41L17.59 5 12 10.59 6.41 5 5 6.41 10.59 12 5 17.59 6.41 19 12 13.41 17.59 19 19 17.59 13.41 12z"/></svg>`
23	const ICON_SEND = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M2.01 21L23 12 2.01 3 2 10l15 2-15 2z"/></svg>`
24	
25	export class RavenChat extends HTMLElement {
26	  static get observedAttributes(): string[] {
27	    return [
28	      'api-key',
29	      'api-url',
30	      'theme-color',
31	      'avatar-url',
32	      'welcome-text',
33	      'position',
34	    ]
35	  }
36	
37	  // ---- Internal state ----
38	  private _isOpen = false
39	  private _messages: ChatMessage[] = []
40	  private _isStreaming = false
41	  private _abortController: AbortController | null = null
42	
43	  // ---- Shadow DOM element refs ----
44	  private _panel!: HTMLElement
45	  private _bubble!: HTMLButtonElement
46	  private _messagesContainer!: HTMLElement
47	  private _typingIndicator!: HTMLElement
48	  private _input!: HTMLTextAreaElement
49	  private _sendBtn!: HTMLButtonElement
50	  private _welcomeEl!: HTMLElement
51	
52	  constructor() {
53	    super()
54	    this.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' })
55	  }
56	
57	  // ---- Lifecycle ----
58	
59	  connectedCallback(): void {
60	    this._render()
61	    this._bindEvents()
62	    // Ensure position attribute has a default
63	    if (!this.getAttribute('position')) {
64	      this.setAttribute('position', DEFAULT_POSITION)
65	    }
66	  }
67	
68	  disconnectedCallback(): void {
69	    this._abortController?.abort()
70	  }
71	
72	  attributeChangedCallback(
73	    name: string,
74	    oldVal: string | null,
75	    newVal: string | null,
76	  ): void {
77	    if (oldVal === newVal) return
78	
79	    // Re-render only when theme changes (requires full CSS rebuild)
80	    if (name === 'theme-color') {
81	      this._render()
82	      this._bindEvents()
83	      return
84	    }
85	
86	    // Update specific parts without full re-render
87	    if (name === 'welcome-text' && this._welcomeEl) {
88	      this._welcomeEl.textContent = newVal ?? DEFAULT_WELCOME_TEXT
89	    }
90	
91	    if (name === 'avatar-url') {
92	      const avatar = this.shadowRoot?.querySelector(
93	        '.rc-header-avatar',
94	      ) as HTMLImageElement | null
95	      if (avatar && newVal) {
96	        avatar.src = newVal
97	      }
98	    }
99	  }
100	
101	  // ---- Getters for attributes ----
102	
103	  private get _apiKey(): string {
104	    return this.getAttribute('api-key') ?? ''
105	  }
106	
107	  private get _apiUrl(): string {
108	    return this.getAttribute('api-url') ?? ''
109	  }
110	
111	  private get _themeColor(): string {
112	    return this.getAttribute('theme-color') ?? DEFAULT_THEME_COLOR
113	  }
114	
115	  private get _avatarUrl(): string {
116	    return this.getAttribute('avatar-url') ?? ''
117	  }
118	
119	  private get _welcomeText(): string {
120	    return this.getAttribute('welcome-text') ?? DEFAULT_WELCOME_TEXT
121	  }
122	
123	  // ---- Rendering ----
124	
125	  private _render(): void {
126	    const shadow = this.shadowRoot!
127	
128	    const avatarHtml = this._avatarUrl
129	      ? `<img class="rc-header-avatar" src="${this._escapeHtml(this._avatarUrl)}" alt="Assistant avatar" />`
130	      : `<div class="rc-header-avatar"></div>`
131	
132	    shadow.innerHTML = `
133	      <style>${chatStyles(this._themeColor)}</style>
134	
135	      <div class="rc-panel" role="dialog" aria-label="Chat with assistant">
136	        <div class="rc-header">
137	          ${avatarHtml}
138	          <div class="rc-header-info">
139	            <div class="rc-header-title">Raven Assistant</div>
140	            <div class="rc-header-subtitle">Typically replies instantly</div>
141	          </div>
142	          <button class="rc-close-btn" aria-label="Close chat" type="button">
143	            ${ICON_CLOSE}
144	          </button>
145	        </div>
146	
147	        <div class="rc-messages" aria-live="polite" aria-relevant="additions">
148	          <div class="rc-welcome">${this._escapeHtml(this._welcomeText)}</div>
149	          <div class="rc-typing" aria-label="Assistant is typing">
150	            <span class="rc-typing-dot"></span>
151	            <span class="rc-typing-dot"></span>
152	            <span class="rc-typing-dot"></span>
153	          </div>
154	        </div>
155	
156	        <div class="rc-input-area">
157	          <textarea
158	            class="rc-input"
159	            placeholder="Type a message..."
160	            rows="1"
161	            aria-label="Message input"
162	          ></textarea>
163	          <button class="rc-send-btn" aria-label="Send message" type="button">
164	            ${ICON_SEND}
165	          </button>
166	        </div>
167	
168	        <div class="rc-powered">Powered by <a href="https://ravenapp.dev" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Raven</a></div>
169	      </div>
170	
171	      <button class="rc-bubble" aria-label="Open chat" aria-expanded="false" type="button">
172	        ${ICON_CHAT}
173	      </button>
174	    `
175	
176	    // Cache element refs
177	    this._panel = shadow.querySelector('.rc-panel') as HTMLElement
178	    this._bubble = shadow.querySelector('.rc-bubble') as HTMLButtonElement
179	    this._messagesContainer = shadow.querySelector(
180	      '.rc-messages',
181	    ) as HTMLElement
182	    this._typingIndicator = shadow.querySelector('.rc-typing') as HTMLElement
183	    this._input = shadow.querySelector('.rc-input') as HTMLTextAreaElement
184	    this._sendBtn = shadow.querySelector('.rc-send-btn') as HTMLButtonElement
185	    this._welcomeEl = shadow.querySelector('.rc-welcome') as HTMLElement
186	
187	    // Restore existing messages after re-render
188	    this._restoreMessages()
189	  }
190	
191	  private _restoreMessages(): void {
192	    if (this._messages.length > 0) {
193	      this._welcomeEl.style.display = 'none'
194	    }
195	
196	    for (const msg of this._messages) {
197	      this._appendMessageBubble(msg.role, msg.content)
198	    }
199	
200	    if (this._isOpen) {
201	      this._panel.classList.add('open')
202	      this._bubble.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true')
203	    }
204	  }
205	
206	  // ---- Event binding ----
207	
208	  private _bindEvents(): void {
209	    // Toggle chat panel
210	    this._bubble.addEventListener('click', () => this._togglePanel())
211	
212	    // Close button
213	    const closeBtn = this.shadowRoot!.querySelector(
214	      '.rc-close-btn',
215	    ) as HTMLButtonElement
216	    closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => this._togglePanel(false))
217	
218	    // Send on button click
219	    this._sendBtn.addEventListener('click', () => this._handleSend())
220	
221	    // Send on Enter (Shift+Enter for newline)
222	    this._input.addEventListener('keydown', (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
223	      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
224	        e.preventDefault()
225	        this._handleSend()
226	      }
227	    })
228	
229	    // Auto-resize textarea
230	    this._input.addEventListener('input', () => {
231	      this._input.style.height = 'auto'
232	      this._input.style.height =
233	        Math.min(this._input.scrollHeight, 100) + 'px'
234	    })
235	  }
236	
237	  // ---- Panel toggle ----
238	
239	  private _togglePanel(forceState?: boolean): void {
240	    this._isOpen = forceState !== undefined ? forceState : !this._isOpen
241	
242	    if (this._isOpen) {
243	      this._panel.classList.add('open')
244	      this._bubble.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true')
245	      // Focus input after panel animation
246	      setTimeout(() => this._input.focus(), 260)
247	    } else {
248	      this._panel.classList.remove('open')
249	      this._bubble.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false')
250	    }
251	  }
252	
253	  // ---- Message handling ----
254	
255	  private _handleSend(): void {
256	    const text = this._input.value.trim()
257	    if (!text || this._isStreaming) return
258	
259	    // Hide welcome text on first message
260	    if (this._messages.length === 0) {
261	      this._welcomeEl.style.display = 'none'
262	    }
263	
264	    // Add user message
265	    this._messages.push({ role: 'user', content: text })
266	    this._appendMessageBubble('user', text)
267	
268	    // Clear input and reset height
269	    this._input.value = ''
270	    this._input.style.height = 'auto'
271	
272	    // Show typing indicator
273	    this._setTyping(true)
274	    this._isStreaming = true
275	    this._sendBtn.disabled = true
276	
277	    // Create abort controller for this request
278	    this._abortController = new AbortController()
279	
280	    // Prepare a mutable assistant message
281	    let assistantContent = ''
282	
283	    sendMessage({
284	      apiUrl: this._apiUrl,
285	      apiKey: this._apiKey,
286	      message: text,
287	      history: [...this._messages],
288	      signal: this._abortController.signal,
289	      onChunk: (chunk: string) => {
290	        // Hide typing indicator on first chunk
291	        if (assistantContent === '') {
292	          this._setTyping(false)
293	          this._appendMessageBubble('assistant', '')
294	        }
295	        assistantContent += chunk
296	        this._updateLastAssistantMessage(assistantContent)
297	        this._scrollToBottom()
298	      },
299	      onDone: () => {
300	        this._messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: assistantContent })
301	        this._isStreaming = false
302	        this._sendBtn.disabled = false
303	        this._setTyping(false)
304	        this._scrollToBottom()
305	      },
306	      onError: (error: Error) => {
307	        console.error('[raven-chat]', error)
308	        this._setTyping(false)
309	        this._isStreaming = false
310	        this._sendBtn.disabled = false
311	
312	        const errorText = 'Sorry, something went wrong. Please try again.'
313	        this._messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: errorText })
314	        this._appendMessageBubble('assistant', errorText)
315	        this._scrollToBottom()
316	      },
317	    })
318	
319	    this._scrollToBottom()
320	  }
321	
322	  // ---- DOM helpers ----
323	
324	  private _appendMessageBubble(role: 'user' | 'assistant', text: string): void {
325	    const el = document.createElement('div')
326	    el.className = `rc-msg rc-msg--${role}`
327	    el.textContent = text
328	
329	    // Insert before the typing indicator
330	    this._messagesContainer.insertBefore(el, this._typingIndicator)
331	    this._scrollToBottom()
332	  }
333	
334	  private _updateLastAssistantMessage(content: string): void {
335	    const allMsgs = this._messagesContainer.querySelectorAll('.rc-msg--assistant')
336	    const last = allMsgs[allMsgs.length - 1]
337	    if (last) {
338	      last.textContent = content
339	    }
340	  }
341	
342	  private _setTyping(visible: boolean): void {
343	    if (visible) {
344	      this._typingIndicator.classList.add('visible')
345	    } else {
346	      this._typingIndicator.classList.remove('visible')
347	    }
348	    this._scrollToBottom()
349	  }
350	
351	  private _scrollToBottom(): void {
352	    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
353	      this._messagesContainer.scrollTop =
354	        this._messagesContainer.scrollHeight
355	    })
356	  }
357	
358	  private _escapeHtml(str: string): string {
359	    const div = document.createElement('div')
360	    div.textContent = str
361	    return div.innerHTML
362	  }
363	}
364	
```

1	/**
2	 * Chat API client for <raven-chat>.
3	 *
4	 * Handles SSE streaming communication with the Raven backend.
5	 * Currently ships with a mock implementation that simulates
6	 * word-by-word streaming via setTimeout.
7	 */
8	
9	import { pipe, map, filter } from 'remeda'
10	
11	export interface ChatMessage {
12	  role: 'user' | 'assistant'
13	  content: string
14	}
15	
16	export interface SendMessageOptions {
17	  apiUrl: string
18	  apiKey: string
19	  message: string
20	  history?: ChatMessage[]
21	  onChunk: (chunk: string) => void
22	  onDone: () => void
23	  onError: (error: Error) => void
24	  signal?: AbortSignal
25	}
26	
27	/**
28	 * Sends a chat message and streams the response back chunk-by-chunk.
29	 *
30	 * TODO: Replace mock implementation with real SSE fetch once the
31	 *       backend streaming endpoint is available.
32	 *
33	 * Real implementation will:
34	 *   1. POST to `${apiUrl}/v1/chat/stream`
35	 *   2. Read the response body as a ReadableStream
36	 *   3. Parse SSE `data:` frames and call `onChunk` for each token
37	 *   4. Call `onDone` when the stream ends (receives `[DONE]`)
38	 */
39	export function sendMessage(options: SendMessageOptions): void {
40	  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars
41	  const { message, onChunk, onDone, onError: _onError, signal } = options
42	
43	  // --- Mock streaming implementation ---
44	  const mockResponses = [
45	    `That's a great question! Let me help you with that. Based on what you've asked about "${truncate(message, 40)}", here's what I can tell you.`,
46	    `I'd be happy to assist! Regarding "${truncate(message, 40)}", there are a few important things to consider. Let me walk you through the key points.`,
47	    `Thanks for reaching out! Here's some helpful information about "${truncate(message, 40)}". I'll break it down step by step for you.`,
48	  ]
49	
50	  const responseText =
51	    mockResponses[Math.floor(Math.random() * mockResponses.length)]
52	  const words = responseText.split(' ')
53	  let index = 0
54	  let cancelled = false
55	
56	  const onAbort = () => {
57	    cancelled = true
58	  }
59	  signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
60	
61	  function emitNextWord() {
62	    if (cancelled) {
63	      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
64	      return
65	    }
66	
67	    if (index < words.length) {
68	      const word = (index === 0 ? '' : ' ') + words[index]
69	      onChunk(word)
70	      index++
71	      // Simulate variable network latency (30-80ms per word)
72	      const delay = 30 + Math.random() * 50
73	      setTimeout(emitNextWord, delay)
74	    } else {
75	      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
76	      onDone()
77	    }
78	  }
79	
80	  // Small initial delay to simulate network round-trip
81	  setTimeout(emitNextWord, 300)
82	
83	  // TODO: Real SSE implementation outline:
84	  //

131	  //   }

133	
134	  // For the mock, simulate potential errors on bad config
135	  if (!options.apiUrl || !options.apiKey) {
136	    // In mock mode we still stream, but log a warning
137	    // In production this would call onError immediately
138	    console.warn(
139	      '[raven-chat] Missing apiUrl or apiKey — using mock responses.',
140	    )
141	  }
142	}

144	/**
145	 * Parse an SSE line buffer into individual data payloads.
146	 * Exported for unit-testing.
147	 */
148	export function parseSSELines(raw: string): string[] {
149	  return pipe(
150	    raw.split('\n'),
151	    map((line) => line.trim()),
152	    filter((line) => line.startsWith('data: ')),
153	    map((line) => line.slice(6)),
154	    filter((payload) => payload !== '[DONE]'),
155	  )
156	}
157	
158	function truncate(str: string, maxLen: number): string {
159	  if (str.length <= maxLen) return str
160	  return str.slice(0, maxLen) + '...'
161	}
162

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a thorough report:

## Raven AI Worker - Comprehensive Codebase Overview

I've thoroughly explored the Python AI worker codebase. Here's a complete breakdown for LiveKit Agents integration:

### 1. OVERALL DIRECTORY STRUCTURE

**File layout:**
```
raven_worker/
├── __init__.py                 # Version 0.1.0
├── __main__.py                 # Entry point
├── server.py                   # gRPC server setup & servicers
├── config.py                   # Environment-based settings
├── crypto.py                   # AES-256-GCM key decryption
├── telemetry.py               # OpenTelemetry initialization
│
├── services/                   # gRPC servicer implementations
│   ├── embedding.py           # GetEmbedding RPC handler
│   └── rag.py                 # QueryRAG streaming RPC handler
│
├── providers/                  # Multi-provider embedding abstraction
│   ├── base.py                # EmbeddingProvider protocol
│   ├── registry.py            # Provider factory + caching
│   ├── openai_provider.py     # OpenAI embedding (with retry)
│   ├── cohere_provider.py     # Cohere embedding
│   └── anthropic_provider.py  # Anthropic embedding
│
├── retrieval/                  # Hybrid search & fusion
│   ├── vector_search.py       # pgvector cosine distance
│   ├── bm25_search.py         # PostgreSQL full-text search
│   ├── rrf.py                 # Reciprocal Rank Fusion
│   └── reranker.py            # Cohere Rerank API
│
├── processors/                 # Document processing
│   ├── parser.py              # LiteParse CLI integration
│   ├── chunker.py             # RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter
│   └── scraper.py             # Web scraping
│
├── generated/                  # Proto-generated code
│   ├── ai_worker_pb2.py       # Message definitions
│   └── ai_worker_pb2_grpc.py  # Service stubs
│
└── ee/                         # Enterprise features
    ├── analytics/
    └── connectors/
```

### 2. gRPC SERVICES & PROTO DEFINITIONS

**Proto file location:** `/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/raven/proto/ai_worker.proto`

**Service definition:**
```protobuf
service AIWorker {
  rpc ParseAndEmbed(ParseRequest) returns (ParseResponse);
  rpc QueryRAG(RAGRequest) returns (stream RAGChunk);
  rpc GetEmbedding(EmbeddingRequest) returns (EmbeddingResponse);
}
```

**Message types:**
- **RAGRequest**: `query`, `org_id`, `kb_ids[]`, `session_id`, `filters{}`, `model`, `provider`
- **RAGChunk**: `text`, `is_final`, `sources[]` (streaming response)
- **Source**: `document_id`, `document_name`, `chunk_text`, `score`
- **EmbeddingRequest**: `text`, `org_id`, `model`, `provider`
- **EmbeddingResponse**: `embedding[]`, `dimensions`

**Server setup** (`server.py` lines 73-127):
- Uses `grpc.aio.server()` with async Python
- Registers `AIWorkerServicer` combining both `EmbeddingServicer` and `RAGServicer`
- Enables gRPC reflection for debugging
- Health checking via `grpc_health.v1`
- Graceful shutdown on SIGINT/SIGTERM (5-second grace period)
- Optional OpenTelemetry interceptor

**Code generation:** Script at `scripts/generate_proto.sh` uses `grpc_tools.protoc` to generate Python stubs from proto file.

### 3. RAG PIPELINE - QUERY FLOW

**Location:** `raven_worker/services/rag.py` (448 lines)

**Complete pipeline (lines 224-344):**

1. **Cache check** (lines 160-183)
   - Single KB queries only
   - Key format: `raven:cache:rag:{sha256(kb_id:normalized_query)}`
   - TTL: 3600 seconds (1 hour)
   - Backend: Valkey/Redis with graceful degradation

2. **Query embedding** (line 237)
   - Calls provider's `embed()` method
   - Uses BYOK provider from database

3. **Hybrid search in parallel** (lines 244-247)
   - **Vector search** (`vector_search.py`):
     - Uses pgvector `<=>` (cosine distance) operator
     - Converts list of floats to `[0.1,0.2,...]` string format
     - SQL: `1 - (e.embedding <=> $1::vector)` for similarity score
     - Limit: 20 results by default
   
   - **BM25 full-text search** (`bm25_search.py`):
     - PostgreSQL `ts_rank_cd()` function
     - Supports English stemming
     - SQL: `plainto_tsquery()` for query parsing

4. **Reciprocal Rank Fusion** (line 256)
   - Merges vector + BM25 rankings
   - Formula: `score(d) = Σ 1/(k + rank)` where k=60
   - Returns top 10 results

5. **Fetch full chunk content** (lines 268-289)
   - SQL query fetches: id, content, heading, document_id, document_name
   - Preserves RRF ordering

6. **Optional re-ranking** (lines 292-304)
   - Cohere Rerank API (if `filters["rerank"] == "cohere"`)
   - Gracefully degrades if unavailable
   - Returns top 5 re-ranked chunks

7. **Build context** (lines 306-313)
   - Concatenates chunk heading + content
   - Format: `[1] Heading\nContent\n\n[2] ...`

8. **Stream LLM completion** (lines 338-341)
   - Provider-specific streaming:
     - **OpenAI**: `AsyncOpenAI` client, system + user messages
     - **Anthropic**: `AsyncAnthropic.messages.stream()`, max_tokens=2048
   - Yields one token per `RAGChunk` message (non-final)

9. **Final chunk with sources** (line 344)
   - `is_final=True`, contains source list with metadata

10. **Cache storage** (lines 207-214)
    - Stores full response as JSON: `{"text": "...", "sources": [...], "model": "..."}`

**Key characteristics:**
- All async/await based
- Connection pooling via `asyncpg`
- Row-level security via PostgreSQL GUC `app.current_org_id`
- Structured logging with `structlog`

### 4. DEPENDENCIES (pyproject.toml)

**Core dependencies (lines 10-31):**
- `grpcio>=1.69.0`, `grpcio-reflection>=1.69.0`, `grpcio-health-checking>=1.69.0`
- `protobuf>=5.29.0`, `pydantic-settings>=2.7.0`
- `structlog>=24.4.0` (structured logging)
- `opentelemetry-{api,sdk,exporter-otlp,instrumentation-grpc}` (tracing)
- `httpx>=0.27.0`, `beautifulsoup4>=4.12.0` (HTTP + HTML parsing)
- `langchain-text-splitters>=0.3.0` (text chunking)
- `openai>=1.0.0`, `cohere>=5.0.0`, `anthropic>=0.40.0` (LLM APIs)
- `asyncpg>=0.30.0` (PostgreSQL async)
- `tenacity>=9.0.0` (retry logic)
- `cryptography>=42.0.0` (AES-256-GCM)
- `redis>=5.0.0` (Valkey/Redis client)

**Dev dependencies (lines 34-41):**
- `grpcio-tools>=1.69.0` (proto compilation)
- `mypy>=1.13.0`, `pytest>=8.3.0`, `pytest-asyncio>=0.25.0`, `pytest-cov>=6.0.0`
- `ruff>=0.9.0` (linting/formatting)

### 5. EXTERNAL SERVICE CONNECTIONS

**PostgreSQL** (database):
- Connection URL from `RAVEN_DATABASE_URL` env var (default: `postgresql://raven:raven@localhost:5432/raven`)
- Uses `asyncpg.create_pool()` (lazy initialization on first RPC call)
- Connections from pool with RLS GUC: `SELECT set_config('app.current_org_id', org_id, false)`
- Tables accessed:
  - `chunks` - document content
  - `embeddings` - vector embeddings
  - `documents` - document metadata
  - `llm_provider_configs` - encrypted BYOK keys

**Valkey/Redis** (response caching):
- URL from `RAVEN_VALKEY_URL` env var (default: `redis://localhost:6379/0`)
- Uses `aioredis.from_url()` (lazy + graceful degradation)
- Operations: `GET`, `SETEX` with TTL
- Namespace: `raven:cache:rag:` prefix

**LLM APIs** (via BYOK):
- **OpenAI**: `AsyncOpenAI` client, base_url configurable
- **Anthropic**: `AsyncAnthropic` client
- **Cohere**: For embeddings + re-ranking

**OpenTelemetry** (optional tracing):
- OTLP gRPC exporter
- Endpoint from `RAVEN_OTEL_ENDPOINT` (no-op if None)
- Enabled via `RAVEN_OTEL_ENABLED` flag

### 6. ENTRY POINT & STARTUP

**Entry point:** `raven_worker/__main__.py` (lines 1-13)
```python
import asyncio
from raven_worker.server import serve

def main() -> None:
    asyncio.run(serve())

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**Command:** `python -m raven_worker`

**Startup sequence** (`server.py`, `serve()` function lines 73-127):

1. Configure structured logging with `structlog` (lines 53-70)
   - Log level from `RAVEN_LOG_LEVEL` (default: INFO)
   - Processors: contextvars, log_level, StackInfoRenderer, TimeStamper, ConsoleRenderer

2. Initialize OpenTelemetry if endpoint configured (line 79)

3. Create gRPC server with optional telemetry interceptor (lines 82-84)

4. Register servicer + health checking + reflection (lines 87-103)

5. Bind to `[::]:{RAVEN_GRPC_PORT}` insecure port (line 106)
   - Default: 50051
   - Max workers: `RAVEN_GRPC_MAX_WORKERS` (default: 10)

6. Start server and wait for shutdown signal (lines 109-126)

### 7. AUDIO/VOICE/STREAMING CODE

**Currently:** None exists. Streaming is only for LLM token responses via `async for` in RAG pipeline.

**Relevant streaming patterns already implemented:**
- `RAGServicer.query()` method uses `async def ... AsyncIterator[ai_worker_pb2.RAGChunk]`
- `_stream_llm()` helper yields chunks token-by-token via `async for`
- Both OpenAI and Anthropic streaming clients already integrated

**Integration point for LiveKit Agents:**
- Would add new RPC method to proto (e.g., `rpc StreamAudio(stream AudioFrame) returns (stream AudioResponse)`)
- New servicer class to handle real-time audio processing
- Existing infrastructure: async/await, streaming, structured logging ready

### 8. CONFIGURATION & ENVIRONMENT PATTERNS

**Configuration class** (`config.py` lines 6-26):

```python
class Settings(BaseSettings):
    grpc_port: int = 50051
    grpc_max_workers: int = 10
    database_url: str = "postgresql://raven:raven@localhost:5432/raven"
    valkey_url: str = "redis://localhost:6379/0"
    otel_endpoint: str | None = None
    otel_enabled: bool = False
    log_level: str = "INFO"
    liteparse_path: str = "liteparse"
    encryption_key: str = ""  # base64-encoded 32-byte AES key
    
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_prefix="RAVEN_")
```

**Environment variable patterns:**
- Prefix: `RAVEN_` (e.g., `RAVEN_GRPC_PORT=50052`)
- Pydantic auto-converts types (int, bool, str)
- Optional encryption key for BYOK decryption

**Secrets handling:**
- API keys stored encrypted in database (AES-256-GCM)
- Decryption done via `decrypt_api_key()` (`crypto.py` lines 19-47)
- IV + ciphertext fetched from `llm_provider_configs` table

### 9. TEST STRUCTURE

**Test files** (tests/ directory):

| File | Purpose | Key Test Types |
|------|---------|-----------------|
| `conftest.py` | Shared fixtures | Mock gRPC context, disable RAG cache autouse |
| `test_server.py` | Config & imports | Settings defaults, module imports |
| `test_rag_service.py` | RAG pipeline | RRF fusion, vector/BM25 search, cache logic |
| `test_embedding_servicer.py` | Embedding RPC | Provider lookup, error handling |
| `test_embedding_providers.py` | Provider implementations | OpenAI/Cohere/Anthropic embeddings |
| `test_registry.py` | Provider caching | Cache hits, provider factory |
| `test_chunker.py` | Text splitting | Chunk size, overlap, metadata |
| `test_parser.py` | Document parsing | LiteParse integration |
| `test_scraper.py` | Web scraping | HTTP fetching, HTML parsing |
| `test_cache.py` | Response caching | Cache key generation, Redis ops |
| `test_telemetry.py` | OpenTelemetry | OTEL initialization, interceptors |

**Test configuration** (`pyproject.toml` lines 74-76):
```ini
[tool.pytest.ini_options]
testpaths = ["tests"]
asyncio_mode = "auto"
```

**Fixture patterns** (`conftest.py`):
- `@pytest.fixture` for Settings
- `@pytest.fixture` for mock gRPC context
- `@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)` for cache disabling in tests

**Mock patterns** (from test files):
- `AsyncMock` for async functions
- `MagicMock` with `@asynccontextmanager` for asyncpg pool
- `patch()` for monkey-patching

### 10. KEY ARCHITECTURAL PATTERNS

**1. Provider Registry Pattern** (`providers/registry.py`):
- Module-level cache: `_provider_cache[org_id, provider_name, model] → EmbeddingProvider`
- Lazy DB lookup only on cache miss
- Supports OpenAI, Cohere, Anthropic with single protocol

**2. Protocol-Based Abstraction** (`providers/base.py`):
```python
class EmbeddingProvider(Protocol):
    async def embed(self, text: str) -> list[float]: ...
    @property
    def dimensions(self) -> int: ...
    @property
    def model_name(self) -> str: ...
```

**3. Graceful Degradation**:
- Cache failures → pipeline continues
- Redis unavailable → skips cache
- Cohere reranking unavailable → uses RRF results
- OTEL not installed → no-op tracer

**4. Row-Level Security (RLS)**:
- PostgreSQL GUC `app.current_org_id` set per connection
- Applies to all subsequent queries on that connection
- Prevents cross-org data leakage

**5. Async First**:
- All database operations use `asyncpg`
- All I/O (HTTP, Redis, gRPC) is async
- Structured with `asyncio.gather()` for parallelism (vector + BM25 search)

**6. Structured Logging**:
- Every RPC entry/exit logged
- Context binding: `logger.bind(org_id=..., kb_ids=...)`
- Log level configurable via env var

---

## Integration Points for LiveKit Agents

Based on this architecture, LiveKit Agents integration would naturally fit:

1. **New RPC method** in proto + servicer (parallel to QueryRAG)
2. **Audio processor** module (parallel to `processors/parser.py`)
3. **Streaming handler** (leverage existing `async for` patterns)
4. **Real-time embedding** (reuse `providers` registry)
5. **State management** (per-session context, similar to `session_id` in RAGRequest)

The worker is purpose-built for streaming, async workloads with multi-provider abstraction and proper secret handling—ideal foundation for voice AI features.
```

> AGENT

Good context. The issue is about integrating LiveKit Agents SDK into the Python worker so the AI agent can join LiveKit rooms and handle audio. This is purely ai-worker Python work.

The key design decision: LiveKit Agents SDK runs as its own process (it has its own event loop and room management). It doesn't fit neatly inside the existing gRPC server. Two approaches:

**A) Separate entrypoint** — Add a new `raven_worker/agent.py` that runs as a LiveKit Agent worker alongside the existing gRPC server. Docker Compose gets a second Python service (`python-agent`). The agent calls the existing gRPC RAG pipeline internally.

**B) Embedded in existing server** — Add LiveKit room management inside the gRPC server process. Tighter coupling but single process.

**My recommendation: A.** LiveKit Agents SDK is designed to run as a standalone worker process. Fighting that design adds complexity. Two services is cleaner — the agent handles voice, calls the existing RAG gRPC endpoint for knowledge retrieval.

Going with A. Let me build it:

1	"""gRPC server setup with reflection, health checking, and graceful shutdown."""
2	
3	import asyncio
4	import signal
5	
6	import grpc
7	import structlog
8	from grpc_health.v1 import health, health_pb2, health_pb2_grpc
9	from grpc_reflection.v1alpha import reflection
10	
11	from raven_worker.config import settings
12	from raven_worker.generated import ai_worker_pb2, ai_worker_pb2_grpc
13	from raven_worker.services.embedding import EmbeddingServicer
14	from raven_worker.services.rag import RAGServicer
15	from raven_worker.telemetry import get_grpc_server_interceptor, init_telemetry
16	
17	logger = structlog.get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	class AIWorkerServicer(
21	    ai_worker_pb2_grpc.AIWorkerServicer,
22	):
23	    """Combines all AI Worker RPC implementations into a single servicer."""
24	
25	    def __init__(self) -> None:
26	        self._embedding = EmbeddingServicer()
27	        self._rag = RAGServicer()
28	
29	    async def ParseAndEmbed(self, request, context):
30	        """Parse a document and generate embeddings for its chunks."""
31	        logger.info(
32	            "parse_and_embed_request",
33	            document_id=request.document_id,
34	            org_id=request.org_id,
35	            mime_type=request.mime_type,
36	        )
37	        await context.abort(
38	            grpc.StatusCode.UNIMPLEMENTED,
39	            "ParseAndEmbed is not yet implemented. "
40	            "This will integrate LiteParse for document parsing and chunk embedding.",
41	        )
42	
43	    async def QueryRAG(self, request, context):
44	        """Stream RAG results for a query."""
45	        async for chunk in self._rag.query(request, context):
46	            yield chunk
47	
48	    async def GetEmbedding(self, request, context):
49	        """Generate an embedding for a single text input."""
50	        return await self._embedding.get_embedding(request, context)
51	
52	
53	def _configure_logging() -> None:
54	    """Set up structured logging with structlog."""
55	    structlog.configure(
56	        processors=[
57	            structlog.contextvars.merge_contextvars,
58	            structlog.processors.add_log_level,
59	            structlog.processors.StackInfoRenderer(),
60	            structlog.dev.set_exc_info,
61	            structlog.processors.TimeStamper(fmt="iso"),
62	            structlog.dev.ConsoleRenderer(),
63	        ],
64	        wrapper_class=structlog.make_filtering_bound_logger(
65	            structlog.get_level_from_name(settings.log_level),
66	        ),
67	        context_class=dict,
68	        logger_factory=structlog.PrintLoggerFactory(),
69	        cache_logger_on_first_use=True,
70	    )
71	
72	
73	async def serve() -> None:
74	    """Start the gRPC server and block until shutdown is requested."""
75	    _configure_logging()
76	
77	    # Initialise OpenTelemetry (no-op when disabled or no endpoint).
78	    otel_endpoint = settings.otel_endpoint if settings.otel_enabled else None
79	    init_telemetry(service_name="raven-ai-worker", endpoint=otel_endpoint)
80	
81	    # Build gRPC interceptors list.
82	    interceptors: list = [i for i in [get_grpc_server_interceptor()] if i is not None]
83	
84	    server = grpc.aio.server(interceptors=interceptors or None)
85	
86	    # Register AI Worker servicer
87	    ai_worker_pb2_grpc.add_AIWorkerServicer_to_server(AIWorkerServicer(), server)
88	
89	    # Health checking
90	    health_servicer = health.aio.HealthServicer()
91	    health_pb2_grpc.add_HealthServicer_to_server(health_servicer, server)
92	    await health_servicer.set(
93	        "raven.ai.v1.AIWorker",
94	        health_pb2.HealthCheckResponse.SERVING,
95	    )
96	
97	    # Server reflection for development/debugging
98	    service_names = (
99	        ai_worker_pb2.DESCRIPTOR.services_by_name["AIWorker"].full_name,
100	        health_pb2.DESCRIPTOR.services_by_name["Health"].full_name,
101	        reflection.SERVICE_NAME,
102	    )
103	    reflection.enable_server_reflection(service_names, server)
104	
105	    listen_addr = f"[::]:{settings.grpc_port}"
106	    server.add_insecure_port(listen_addr)
107	
108	    logger.info("starting_server", address=listen_addr, max_workers=settings.grpc_max_workers)
109	    await server.start()
110	
111	    # Graceful shutdown on SIGINT / SIGTERM
112	    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
113	    shutdown_event = asyncio.Event()
114	
115	    def _signal_handler() -> None:
116	        logger.info("shutdown_signal_received")
117	        shutdown_event.set()
118	
119	    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
120	        loop.add_signal_handler(sig, _signal_handler)
121	
122	    await shutdown_event.wait()
123	
124	    logger.info("shutting_down", grace_period_seconds=5)
125	    await server.stop(grace=5)
126	    logger.info("server_stopped")
127	
```

1	"""LiveKit Agents voice worker.
2	
3	Runs as a standalone process alongside the gRPC server.
4	Joins LiveKit rooms and handles bidirectional audio with an AI assistant.
5	
6	Usage:
7	    python -m raven_worker.agent
8	"""
9	
10	import asyncio
11	import signal
12	
13	import structlog
14	
15	from raven_worker.config import settings
16	
17	logger = structlog.get_logger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	def _configure_logging() -> None:
21	    """Set up structured logging with structlog."""
22	    structlog.configure(
23	        processors=[
24	            structlog.contextvars.merge_contextvars,
25	            structlog.processors.add_log_level,
26	            structlog.processors.StackInfoRenderer(),
27	            structlog.dev.set_exc_info,
28	            structlog.processors.TimeStamper(fmt="iso"),
29	            structlog.dev.ConsoleRenderer(),
30	        ],
31	        wrapper_class=structlog.make_filtering_bound_logger(
32	            structlog.get_level_from_name(settings.log_level),
33	        ),
34	        context_class=dict,
35	        logger_factory=structlog.PrintLoggerFactory(),
36	        cache_logger_on_first_use=True,
37	    )
38	
39	
40	async def _create_agent_worker():
41	    """Create and configure the LiveKit agent worker."""
42	    try:
43	        from livekit.agents import AutoSubscribe, WorkerOptions, cli
44	        from livekit.agents.voice import AgentSession
45	    except ImportError:
46	        logger.error(
47	            "livekit_agents_not_installed",
48	            hint="Install with: pip install 'livekit-agents>=1.0.0'",
49	        )
50	        raise SystemExit(1)
51	
52	    async def _entrypoint(ctx):
53	        """Called when the agent joins a room."""
54	        logger.info(
55	            "agent_joined_room",
56	            room=ctx.room.name,
57	            participant=ctx.room.local_participant.identity,
58	        )
59	
60	        # Create a voice agent session
61	        # STT and TTS plugins will be added in issues #59 and #60
62	        session = AgentSession()
63	
64	        # TODO (#59): Add STT plugin (Deepgram or faster-whisper)
65	        # TODO (#60): Add TTS plugin (Cartesia or Piper)
66	        # TODO: Connect to RAG pipeline via gRPC for knowledge retrieval
67	        #   channel = grpc.aio.insecure_channel(f"localhost:{settings.grpc_port}")
68	        #   stub = ai_worker_pb2_grpc.AIWorkerStub(channel)
69	
70	        await session.start(room=ctx.room, participant=ctx.room.remote_participants.get(0))
71	
72	        logger.info("agent_session_started", room=ctx.room.name)
73	
74	    return WorkerOptions(
75	        entrypoint_fnc=_entrypoint,
76	        ws_url=settings.livekit_url,
77	        api_key=settings.livekit_api_key,
78	        api_secret=settings.livekit_api_secret,
79	        auto_subscribe=AutoSubscribe.AUDIO_ONLY,
80	    )
81	
82	
83	async def serve() -> None:
84	    """Start the LiveKit agent worker."""
85	    _configure_logging()
86	
87	    logger.info(
88	        "starting_agent_worker",
89	        livekit_url=settings.livekit_url,
90	    )
91	
92	    worker_options = await _create_agent_worker()
93	
94	    from livekit.agents import cli
95	
96	    # Use LiveKit's CLI runner which handles graceful shutdown
97	    cli.run_app(worker_options)
98	
99	
100	def main() -> None:
101	    """Entry point for the agent worker."""
102	    asyncio.run(serve())
103	
104	
105	if __name__ == "__main__":
106	    main()
107

> AGENT

PR #157 is open and queued for auto-merge: https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/pull/157

The key change: replaced the 7 hardcoded `check-success` lines with:
```yaml
- "#check-failure = 0"
- "#check-pending = 0"
```

Now Mergify merges when **all currently-running checks pass** — it doesn't care which ones ran. A Python-only PR skips Go/Docker checks entirely, so those checks never appear, and Mergify sees zero failures and zero pending → auto-merges. Same logic applies in reverse for Go-only or frontend-only PRs.

> AGENT

The problem: all 4 workflows trigger on every PR (no `paths:` on `on:`), so all 7 checks appear as "Expected" for every PR including `.mergify.yml` changes. The step-level `if:` skips the work but still starts the runner.

The fix:
1. Add `paths:` to `on: pull_request:` in each workflow so they only trigger for relevant files
2. Add a tiny always-run `ci-required.yml` workflow as the single required check
3. Update Mergify to require the gate check + `#check-failure = 0`
4. Update branch protection to only require the gate

Let me read the full workflows first:

Now I have the full picture. The fix: add `paths:` to `on: pull_request:` triggers so workflows only run for relevant files, remove the internal `changes` job + step-level `if:` (no longer needed), add a tiny always-run `ci-required.yml` as the single required check, and update branch protection.

I'll add all this to the existing PR #157:

> AGENT

Done. Here's what changed and why it works:

**Before:** All 4 workflows triggered on every PR. They spun up runners, ran the `changes` job, detected no relevant files, then ran the work jobs with all steps skipped. GitHub branch protection required all 7 specific check names — so even for `.mergify.yml` changes, you waited for 7 "Expected" checks.

**After:**
- `ci-required.yml` — always runs, just echoes success. This is now the **only** required check in branch protection.
- `go.yml`, `python.yml`, `frontend.yml`, `docker.yml` — each has `paths:` on `on: pull_request:`. They **don't start at all** unless matching files change. Internal `changes` job and step-level `if:` removed — no longer needed.
- Mergify requires `CI Required / Gate` success + `#check-failure = 0` + `#check-pending = 0`.

Result:
- Python-only PR → Gate + Python CI run. Go/Frontend/Docker never start.
- Go-only PR → Gate + Go CI run. Python/Frontend/Docker never start.

I'm not aware of a feature called "undercover mode" in Claude Code (the CLI) or on claude.ai. 

You might be thinking of one of these features:

1. **Incognito/Private browsing** — If using Claude through a web browser, your browser's private/incognito mode keeps your session history private locally.

2. **Conversation privacy settings** — On claude.ai, you can control whether conversations are shared or kept private in your account settings.

3. **Anonymous/Guest mode** — Some AI services offer guest sessions without requiring login, though Claude typically requires authentication.

4. **Claude Code's sandboxing** — The CLI runs code in isolated environments, which is a security feature but not called "undercover mode."

Could you clarify what you're trying to accomplish? For example:
- Do you want to hide your Claude Code activity?
- Are you looking for privacy settings on claude.ai?
- Do you want to test something without logging your activity?

I can help point you to the right feature once I understand what you need.

1	# Raven — Claude Code Instructions
2	
3	## Pull Request Workflow
4	
5	After creating a PR with `gh pr create`, **always** immediately queue it for auto-merge:
6	
7	```bash
8	gh pr merge <PR_NUMBER> --auto --squash
9	```
10	
11	This queues the PR to squash-merge automatically once all CI checks pass. Do not wait for CI — enqueue immediately after creation.
12	
13	## Branch Naming
14	
15	Use `type/descriptor` format:
16	
17	| Type | When |
18	|------|------|
19	| `feat/` | New feature |
20	| `fix/` | Bug fix |
21	| `refactor/` | Code restructure, no behaviour change |
22	| `ci/` | CI/CD changes |
23	| `chore/` | Tooling, deps, config |
24	| `deps/` | Dependency bumps |
25	
26	## Rules
27	
28	- **Never push directly to `main`** — always use a PR
29	- **Squash merge only** — never regular merge or rebase-merge
30	- **Never use `--no-verify`** — all hooks must pass
31	- **Never amend published commits** — create new commits instead
32
